Binary: pocketbase version 0.40.4  (POCKETBASE_VERSION pinned to 0.40.4)
Fixtures: scripts/make-photo-fixtures.py. iphone.heic: a real iPhone 13 file (iOS 16.0) supplied by Kayden, with real GPS; NOT committed (gitignored).
exiftool 13.50 installed by Kayden at 2026-11-13 13:1x. THIS FILE HOLDS TWO RUNS of test/upload.test.ts: the first (exiftool and HEIC absent: Option A and HEIC tests skipped) and the second (both present, 23 of 23 passed), which starts at the second '=== control: create [clean-1.jpg, gps.jpg], no hook'. Option A and iphone.heic results are in the second run only.
Stand-in tools used for the dumps: exiv2 0.28.9, plus the byte scanner in test/spike/leaks.ts.

=== desktop Chrome canvas re-encode (by hand, headless Chrome 157 on Linux; not a phone)
A page drew a 640x480 canvas and called canvas.toBlob(cb, "image/jpeg", 0.85). Segments of the 3104-byte result, in order:
    APP0 (JFIF), APP2 (ICC_PROFILE), DQT, DQT, SOF0, DHT x4, SOS; ends with FFD9.
No APP1 (EXIF or XMP), no APP13, no COM. So Option B's scan passes it. iPhone Safari and Android Chrome are NOT checked:
web/exif-probe.html prints "EXIF in output" for each, which is the check that matters for Option B.
